  ⎿  Current Plan
     /Users/filip/.claude/plans/idempotent-moseying-lemur.md

     reforge — AI Coding-Agent Benchmark for Your Own Codebase

     Context

     Companies and organizations must decide which AI coding agent/model to adopt
     — frontier tools
     (Claude, GPT) or non-frontier ones (Gemini, Kimi, etc.). That decision is
     hard because the honest
     question isn't "how does model X score on public benchmarks" but "how well
     does agent X do work
     against our own codebase." Two capabilities matter most:

     1. Replication — reproduce existing features/environments/applications using
     context already in
     the codebase (e.g. "create staging + test environments mirroring prod/dev":
     did it catch all
     dependencies? how accurately?).
     2. New-feature implementation — build net-new functionality with little/no
     prior context
     (e.g. "add an analytics dashboard from internal events data").

     reforge is a self-hosted, Docker-based, model-agnostic CLI (no UI, pure CLI
     + library, Python)
     that runs a company's own tasks against any agent and produces reproducible,
     comparable scores. It
     mirrors proven patterns from SWE-bench (task = repo@commit + problem + gold
     patch +
     FAIL_TO_PASS/PASS_TO_PASS, containerized harness, gold self-verification,
     results JSON) and
     Terminal-bench (task-bundle-as-directory with Dockerfile + verifier +
     oracle; strict
     agent/harness separation), and adds two things the problem needs that
     neither has: a
     dependency-coverage scorer and a unified two-category task schema.

     Locked decisions: Python · BYO-agent adapter interface (+ reference
     adapters) · hybrid scoring
     (deterministic tests + dependency-coverage + LLM-judge rubric) · both task
     categories in v1 ·
     self-hosted, Apache 2.0 · GitHub with protected main, Dependabot,
     CodeQL/Trivy, CI gates
     (patterned on the Burnwise/burnwise repo).

     Reference patterns reused (do not reinvent)

     - SWE-bench containerized eval + F2P/P2P resolved criterion + gold-patch
     self-verification.
     - Terminal-bench task bundle (dir with Dockerfile + verifier + oracle) +
     agent/adapter split.
     - Burnwise GitHub setup
     (/Users/filip/Documents/Personal/Burnwise/burnwise/.github/): reuse its
     codeql.yml, security.yml (Trivy SARIF + fixable CRITICAL/HIGH gate),
     dependabot.yml
     (npm→pip, docker, github-actions), issue/PR templates, config.yml,
     SECURITY.md, release.yml,
     CONTRIBUTING.md, CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md (Contributor Covenant 2.1), Makefile +
     .gitignore style.
     Adapt Node→Python (ruff/mypy/pytest instead of eslint/tsc/playwright).

     Package layout (src/ layout, package reforge, CLI reforge)

     reforge/
     ├── pyproject.toml (hatchling, PEP 621, entry points) · LICENSE (Apache 2.0)
     · NOTICE · README.md
     ├── CONTRIBUTING.md · CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md · SECURITY.md · Makefile ·
     .gitignore · .pre-commit-config.yaml
     ├── .github/{workflows/{ci.yml,codeql.yml,security.yml,release.yml},dependab
     ot.yml,
     │
     ISSUE_TEMPLATE/*,pull_request_template.md,config.yml,CODEOWNERS}
     ├── docs/ (architecture.md + Mermaid diagrams, task-authoring.md,
     adapter-authoring.md)
     ├── src/reforge/
     │   ├── cli.py            # Typer app, thin — delegates to services
     │   ├── config.py         # pydantic-settings: registries, dirs, secrets
     │   ├── spec/             # models.py (pydantic v2 TaskSpec), loader.py,
     validate.py, schema.py (JSON Schema export)
     │   ├── dataset/          # loader.py (local dir + HF), registry.py
     │   ├── workspace/        # source.py (git@sha|local|tarball via subprocess
     git), prepare.py (copy, strip_git, hidden_paths)
     │   ├── runtime/          # base.py (ContainerRuntime ABC +
     ContainerHandle), docker_runtime.py, image.py, limits.py
     │   ├── adapters/         # base.py (AgentAdapter ABC), registry.py (entry
     points), process.py, claude_code.py, aider.py, api_agent.py, noop.py,
     gold.py
     │   ├── runner/           # orchestrator.py (ThreadPoolExecutor),
     task_runner.py (per-task lifecycle), artifacts.py, run_context.py
     │   ├── scoring/          # base.py (Scorer ABC), tests.py, log_parsers.py,
     dependency.py, judge.py, compose.py
     │   ├── report/           # models.py, aggregate.py, render.py
     (rich/markdown/json)
     │   ├── llm/              # client.py (provider-agnostic + rate limiter),
     cost.py (pricing table)
     │   └── utils/            # logging.py (structlog), hashing.py, errors.py
     ├── tasks/                # sample-replication-*, sample-newfeature-*
     (shipped dataset)
     └── tests/{unit,integration,fixtures/tiny-task}

     (resolved == true); noop adapter → resolved == false, F2P==0; concurrency
     isolation smoke.
     - Live E2E via subagents: run reforge run on the sample tasks using a
     subagent-backed adapter
     as the test-subject LLM — confirms the full pipeline and produces a real
     leaderboard row.
     - Manual E2E: reforge run --dataset ./tasks --adapter api_agent --model 
     <frontier> --run-id smoke
     then --model <non-frontier>, and reforge report --compare to confirm
     cross-model leaderboard.
     - CI green on ruff+mypy+unit+Docker gold-verify before any merge to main.

⏺ Plan written to the plan file. Summary:

  Core insight: reforge is already domain-agnostic — a "cloud infra" or "devops"
  task is just a Dockerfile + verifier + detectors. So this milestone makes the
  existing extensibility labelable and pluggable rather than hardcoding task
  types.

  Limitations found: fixed 2-value category enum; hardcoded scorers + fixed
  weights model; detectors are a private dict (not third-party pluggable); no
  project config file (and config.py isn't even wired in); no CI gate exit code;
  no credentialed-task support; no scaffolder; only 2 samples.

  Five tracks (one PR each, all ponytail-scoped):
  - A. Taxonomy — category → free string + tags; existing tasks stay valid
  (StrEnum was already str).
  - B. Pluggable scoring — entry-point groups for scorers + detectors (mirror
  the adapter registry), weights become a dict (same YAML shape), plus built-in
  detectors for k8s / JS-TS / Go / package manifests.
  - C. Config + CI gate — reforge.toml via stdlib tomllib, and run --fail-under 
  <rate> for adoption gating.
  - D. Credentialed tasks — opt-in --env-passthrough KEY ∩ task allowed_env
  allowlist, security-guarded.
  - E. Cookbook + reforge init — scaffolder + two new gold-verified samples
  (terraform cloud-infra, k8s devops).

  Explicitly deferred as YAGNI: k8s/remote backend, web UI, DB/S3 export,
  distributed queue, vault integration.

  ⎿  Plan saved to: ~/.claude/plans/idempotent-moseying-lemur.md · /plan to edit
     reforge v0.2 — flexibility & extensibility

     Context

     reforge v0.1.1 works end to end: containerized harness, hybrid scoring, BYO
     adapters, Docker/Podman, HF datasets, compare/cost/variance. The question
     now is
     how to make it flexible across domains (cloud infra, devops, AI dev, app
     features, ...) and deployments/settings, without bolting on rigid
     machinery.

     Key realization that shapes this plan: the engine is already 
     domain-agnostic.
     A domain is just a task's Dockerfile + verifier + detectors + rubric. The
     replication-staging-env sample already is a devops/infra task. So this
     milestone
     is about labeling, extensibility, ergonomics, and deployment config — not
     new
     execution paths. Decisions locked with the user: free-form task taxonomy;
     and all
     four tracks below.

     Current limitations (honest list)

     1. Taxonomy is a fixed 2-value enum (replication|new_feature,
     spec/models.py
     Category). Can't label domains; reports only group by those two.
     2. Scorers are hardcoded in runner/task_runner.py (Test/Dependency/Judge)
     with a
     fixed ScoringWeights model. No way to add a domain scorer (e.g. "terraform
     plan
     has no destroy", "security scan clean", "cost under $X") without forking.
     3. Detectors are a module-level dict (scoring/dependency.py _REGISTRY), not
     third-party-pluggable like adapters, and cover only
     python/env/terraform/compose/grep.
     4. No project config file. config.py only has output_root/default_network
     and isn't even wired into the CLI; every run repeats flags.
     5. No CI gate. run always exits 0; can't use reforge as an adoption gate
     ("fail if resolved_rate < 0.8").
     6. No credentialed tasks. Network defaults to none and no host env/secret
     passthrough, so tasks needing real cloud/API access (AWS, etc.) can't run.
     7. No task scaffolder (reforge init was dropped in Phase 1); authoring is
     copy-paste.
     8. Only two sample tasks, so domains beyond them have no template.

     Recommended approach

     Mirror patterns already in the repo: the adapter entry-point registry
     (adapters/registry.py), the detector registry (scoring/dependency.py), the
     report/aggregate.py grouping, and pydantic-settings in config.py. Reuse,
     don't
     reinvent.

     Track A — Free-form taxonomy

     - spec/models.py: replace Category enum with category: str (free-form,
     non-empty; keep replication/new_feature as documented suggestions) and add
     tags: list[str] = []. StrEnum was already a str, so existing task.yaml
     stays valid.
     - Fix the one .value use in runner/task_runner.py (spec.category.value →
     spec.category).
     - report/aggregate.py: by_category already keys on the string, so it just
     works;
     add tags into the per-row grouping only if trivial (else skip — YAGNI).
     - cli.py: optional run --select category=<x> / --tag <t> filter over a
     dataset
     (lazy: one predicate over loaded specs). Ship only if it's a few lines.

     Track B — Pluggable scorers + detectors + more built-ins

     - Detectors: add an entry-point group reforge.detectors, merged with the
     built-in _REGISTRY (mirror adapters/registry.py). list detectors shows
     both.
     - New built-in detectors (small pure fns like the existing ones, in
     scoring/dependency.py): k8s_refs (kind/name/image from manifests),
     js_imports
     (import/require in .js/.ts), go_imports (import blocks in .go),
     package_manifests
     (deps from requirements.txt / package.json / go.mod).
     - Scorers: make weights a dict and scorers extensible.
       - spec/models.py: Scoring.weights: dict[str, float] (same YAML shape as
     today, so
     existing tasks parse). validate.py: dict access instead of attributes.
       - scoring/compose.py: iterate dict(weights) (it already composes over
     present keys).
       - scoring/registry.py (new): entry-point group reforge.scorers for extra
     scorers
     taking TaskScoringContext -> ScorerResult. runner/task_runner.py runs the
     three
     built-ins (unchanged construction) plus any registered scorer whose key has
     weight > 0,
     each already wrapped in the per-scorer try/except added in alpha-hardening.
       - ponytail: keep the built-in three special-cased (judge needs a client);
     the registry
     is only for additional context-only scorers. Full DI for every scorer is
     YAGNI.

     Track C — Config file + CI gate

     - Config file: config.py gains a reforge.toml source via stdlib tomllib
     (also
     read [tool.reforge] in pyproject as fallback). Keys mirror run flags:
     adapter,
     model, judge_model, concurrency, network, runtime, max_cost_usd, output.
     CLI flags
     override the file; file overrides built-in defaults. Wire get_settings()
     into cli.run.
     - CI gate: run --fail-under <rate> → after the run, exit non-zero (code 2)
     if the
     leaderboard's resolved_rate is below the threshold. A few lines at the end
     of cli.run.

     Track D — Credentialed / cloud tasks (security-sensitive)

     - run --env-passthrough KEY (repeatable): forward the named host env vars
     into the task
     container + adapter env. Only explicitly listed keys; default none.
     - spec/models.py Environment.allowed_env: list[str] = []: a task must
     allowlist which
     keys it will accept, so a dataset can't exfiltrate arbitrary host env.
     Effective set =
     --env-passthrough ∩ allowed_env.
     - runner/task_runner.py: pass the resolved env to run_container (already
     accepts env)
     and into AdapterInput.env. Loud log.warning naming the forwarded keys.
     - Docs: never put secrets in task.yaml; use with --network bridge; run on
     isolated hosts.
     - ponytail: no vault/secret-store integration — env passthrough only. Add
     more if asked.

     Track E — Cookbook + reforge init

     - reforge init <id> --category <x> (cli.py): scaffold a task dir (task.yaml
     template,
     Dockerfile stub, verifier/run_tests.sh, gold/). Reuses the tiny-task shape.
     - Cookbook: add two new gold-verified samples covering the requested
     domains —
     cloud-infra-terraform (Dockerfile with terraform; verifier runs terraform 
     validate
       - a plan/checkov assertion; terraform_refs/k8s_refs detectors) and
     devops-k8s (kubectl/kustomize; verifier validates manifests). Each must
     pass
     verify-gold in CI (the existing tasks/* CI loop covers them).
     - ponytail: two new samples, not one-per-domain; the rest are
     community-contributable.
     Skipped: ai-dev sample (define once we have a concrete, verifiable ai-dev
     task).

     Critical files

     - src/reforge/spec/models.py — category→str+tags, weights→dict,
     Environment.allowed_env.
     - src/reforge/scoring/dependency.py — detector entry-point discovery + new
     detectors.
     - src/reforge/scoring/registry.py (new) + runner/task_runner.py — pluggable
     scorers.
     - src/reforge/scoring/compose.py, spec/validate.py — dict weights.
     - src/reforge/config.py + src/reforge/cli.py — config file, --fail-under,
     --env-passthrough, init.
     - tasks/cloud-infra-terraform/, tasks/devops-k8s/ — cookbook samples.
     - Docs: README.md, docs/task-authoring.md, docs/adapter-authoring.md (+
     detector/scorer
     authoring), CHANGELOG.md. Run human-facing docs through the humanizer skill
     (no em-dashes).

     Phasing (one PR per track, feature branch → gated main)

     1. A taxonomy (small, unblocks domain labeling).
     2. B pluggable scorers/detectors + built-ins (the core extensibility
     unlock).
     3. C config file + --fail-under.
     4. D credentialed tasks (security review the diff).
     5. E init + two cookbook samples.

     Verification

     - Unit (Docker-run, per repo convention): free-form category round-trips +
     tags; dict
     weights compose + gating; each new detector on fixtures; scorer-registry
     discovery;
     config-file precedence (flag > file > default);
     --env-passthrough∩allowed_env logic;
     --fail-under exit code.
     - Docker integration: verify-gold on the two new cookbook tasks; a task
     using a
     registered extra scorer resolves; env-passthrough reaches the container
     (dummy key).
     - CLI smoke: reforge init produces a dir that reforge validate accepts;
     list detectors/list adapters show entry-point plugins.
     - Green on ruff+mypy+unit+Docker before merge; bump to v0.2.0 and tag when
     the tracks land.

     Explicitly deferred (YAGNI unless asked)

     Kubernetes/remote execution backend (DOCKER_HOST already gives remote
     Docker), web UI /
     hosted leaderboard (CLI by design), DB/S3 result export (runs/ + JSON is
     enough),
     distributed multi-machine queue, secret-store/vault integration, plugin
     marketplace.

⏺ Plan approved. Let me set up tracking and start Track A. First sync main and
  branch.

      359 +def _filter_specs(specs, category, tags):  # type: ignore[no-u
          +ntyped-def]                                                   
      360 +    """Keep specs matching the category and having all request
          +ed tags."""                                                   
      361 +    if category:                                              
      362 +        specs = [s for s in specs if s.category == category]  
      363 +    if tags:                                                  
      364 +        wanted = set(tags)                                    
      365 +        specs = [s for s in specs if wanted <= set(s.tags)]   
      366 +    return specs                                              
      367 +                                                              
      368 +

      370      if not config:

      216 +    """Run registered plugin scorers the task opted into (weig

          +e.MULTILINE)                                                  
      137 +                                                              
      138 +                                                              
      139 +@register("k8s_refs")                                         
      140 +def detect_k8s_refs(files: dict[str, str]) -> set[str]:       
      141 +    """Kinds, names, and images from Kubernetes manifests.""" 
      142 +    found: set[str] = set()                                   
      143 +    for path, content in files.items():                       
      144 +        if not path.endswith((".yml", ".yaml")):              
      145 +            continue                                          
      146 +        found.update(_K8S_KIND.findall(content))              
      147 +        found.update(_K8S_NAME.findall(content))              
      148 +        for image in _IMAGE.findall(content):                 
      149 +            found.add(image.rsplit("/", 1)[-1])               
      150 +    return found                                              
      151 +                                                              
      152 +                                                              
      153 +_JS_IMPORT = re.compile(r"""(?:from|require\()\s*['"]([^'"]+)[
          +'"]""")                                                       
      154 +                                                              
      155 +                                                              
      156 +@register("js_imports")                                       
      157 +def detect_js_imports(files: dict[str, str]) -> set[str]:     
      158 +    """Modules imported/required by JS/TS files."""           
      159 +    found: set[str] = set()                                   
      160 +    for path, content in files.items():                       
      161 +        if path.endswith((".js", ".jsx", ".ts", ".tsx", ".mjs"
          +)):                                                           
      162 +            found.update(_JS_IMPORT.findall(content))         
      163 +    return found                                              
      164 +                                                              
      165 +                                                              
      166 +_GO_IMPORT = re.compile(r'"([a-zA-Z0-9_./-]+)"')              
      167 +                                                              
      168 +                                                              
      169 +@register("go_imports")                                       
      170 +def detect_go_imports(files: dict[str, str]) -> set[str]:     
      171 +    """Packages imported by Go files (from import lines / bloc
          +ks)."""                                                       
      172 +    found: set[str] = set()                                   
      173 +    for path, content in files.items():                       
      174 +        if not path.endswith(".go"):                          
      175 +            continue                                          
      176 +        for line in content.splitlines():                     
      177 +            stripped = line.strip()                           
      178 +            if stripped.startswith("import ") or (stripped and
          + stripped[0] == '"'):                                         
      179 +                found.update(_GO_IMPORT.findall(stripped))    
      180 +    return found                                              
      181 +                                                              
      182 +                                                              
      183 +@register("package_manifests")                                
      184 +def detect_package_manifests(files: dict[str, str]) -> set[str
          +]:                                                            
      185 +    """Dependency names from requirements.txt, package.json, a
          +nd go.mod."""                                                 
      186 +    found: set[str] = set()                                   
      187 +    for path, content in files.items():                       
      188 +        base = path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]                        
      189 +        if base == "requirements.txt":                        
      190 +            for line in content.splitlines():                 
      191 +                name = re.split(r"[=<>!~;\[ ]", line.strip(), 
          +1)[0]                                                         
      192 +                if name and not name.startswith("#"):         
      193 +                    found.add(name)                           
      194 +        elif base == "package.json":                          
      195 +            found.update(re.findall(r'"([^"]+)"\s*:\s*"[^"]*"'
          +, content))                                                   
      196 +        elif base == "go.mod":                                
      197 +            found.update(re.findall(r"^\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_./-]+)\s
          ++v[0-9]", content, re.MULTILINE))                             
      198 +    return found                                              
      199 +                                                              
      200 +                                                              
      201  @register("grep")
      202  def detect_grep(files: dict[str, str]) -> set[str]:
      203      """Fallback: the raw concatenated text, matched by substri
           ng later."""

  ⎿  Added 19 lines
       99  - `--max-cost-usd N` stops launching tasks once the run's spen
           d reaches your
      100    budget, so a large dataset against a frontier model can't ru
           n away with the bill.
      101  
      102 +## Project config and CI gating                               
      103 +                                                              
      104 +Put run defaults in a `reforge.toml` (or a `[tool.reforge]` ta
          +ble in                                                        
      105 +`pyproject.toml`) so runs aren't flag-soup. Flags override the
          + file; the file                                               
      106 +overrides built-in defaults:                                  
      107 +                                                              
      108 +```toml                                                       
      109 +# reforge.toml                                                
      110 +adapter = "api-agent"                                         
      111 +model = "claude-sonnet-4-6"                                   
      112 +judge_model = "claude-sonnet-4-6"                             
      113 +concurrency = 4                                               
      114 +fail_under = 0.8                                              
      115 +```                                                           
      116 +                                                              
      117 +`reforge run --fail-under 0.8` exits non-zero (code 2) if the 
          +resolved rate is                                              
      118 +below the threshold, so you can wire reforge into CI as an ado
          +ption gate: does                                              
      119 +this model clear our bar on our own tasks?                    
      120 +                                                              
      121  ## Datasets and runtimes
      122  
      123  `--dataset` takes a local directory or a HuggingFace dataset r
           epo of reforge task

⏺ Update(SECURITY.md)
  ⎿  Added 11 lines
      31  credentials or data you care about. If a task needs network acc
          ess (for example to
      32  install packages at build time), grant it deliberately with `--
          network` rather
      33  than leaving it on for every task.
      34 +                                                               
      35 +## Credentialed tasks                                          
      36 +                                                               
      37 +Some tasks (cloud infra, for example) need real credentials. re
         +forge forwards a                                               
      38 +host environment variable into a task only when both sides opt 
         +in: the run passes                                             
      39 +`--env-passthrough KEY` and the task allowlists `KEY` in `envir
         +onment.allowed_env`.                                           
      40 +Nothing is forwarded by default, and reforge logs which variabl
         +es it forwarded.                                               
      41 +                                                               
      42 +Never put secret values in `task.yaml`; it only lists variable 
         +names. Give real                                               
      43 +credentials scoped, short-lived permissions, and run these task
         +s on an isolated                                               
      44 +host with the network they need.

⏺ v0.2.0 shipped. https://github.com/filipjevtic/reforge/releases/tag/v0.2.0

  All five flexibility tracks from the plan are merged (PRs #12–#16) and
  released:

  ┌───────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ Track │                           What shipped                           │
  ├───────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ A     │ Free-form category + tags; run --category/--tag filters          │
  ├───────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │       │ Pluggable scorers + detectors via entry points; dict-keyed       │
  │ B     │ weights; new k8s_refs/js_imports/go_imports/package_manifests    │
  │       │ detectors; list scorers                                          │
  ├───────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ C     │ reforge.toml / [tool.reforge] config defaults; run --fail-under  │
  │       │ CI gate (exit 2)                                                 │
  ├───────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ D     │ Credentialed tasks: --env-passthrough KEY ∩ task allowed_env     │
  │       │ allowlist, security-guarded                                      │
  ├───────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ E     │ reforge init scaffolder; cloud-infra-terraform + devops-k8s      │
  │       │ cookbook samples                                                 │
  └───────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Each landed as its own gated PR through protected main, all checks green.
  Final state: 74 unit + 7 Docker integration tests, all four shipped tasks
  gold-verify, ruff/mypy clean, docs + JSON schema + CHANGELOG updated,
  wheel/sdist attached to the release.

  The core design payoff held up: because the engine was already
  domain-agnostic, "more task types" needed no new execution paths — free-form
  labels plus pluggable detectors/scorers made cloud-infra and devops
  first-class, proven by the two new gold-verified samples. Anyone can now
  reforge init a task in their own domain, register a custom detector or scorer
  as a pip-installable plugin, set defaults in reforge.toml, and wire
  --fail-under into CI as an adoption gate.

  One carryover from before, unchanged: the key-gated live smoke test still
  needs a real ANTHROPIC_API_KEY run to exercise the actual provider SDK path
  end to end.
